Binance now stands as one of the most reliable and popular online exchanges in the market. What has caused the explosion of popularity? The fact that this online exchange has a strong emphasis and focus on altcoins is possibly what drives its loyalty among investors. Some say this site is better than even Coinbase if you want to build a portfolio consisting of lesser-known coins. In fact, Coinbase has access to 46 different cryptocurrencies while Binance boasts over 50. That shows just how seriously Binance takes the concept of altcoins.

As well, Binance prides itself on its selection of great data and charts. Top-quality analysis is an important part of making smart investments and Binance has a whole lot of data points to help you navigate the market. You can find and make many different graphs with the site, all of which will help you become an even better crypto trader.

The consumer fees charged at Binance are not too intense or weighty, either. They are usually about .1% of each transaction. That will encourage new investors to trade and buy without fear and hesitation. It is easy to make a new buy when you are only being charged the most minimal amount possible.

It isn't all great news. There are some drawbacks to Binance. For example, the site is built for more advanced users with more experience in the market and using online exchanges. All of the data and analysis doesn't come easy. It also doesn’t work in 22 states in America, which means that if you happen to live in one of those regions you will be locked out of Binance. However, if you can get your hands on this online exchange and you want to experience all that crypto has to offer - especially if you are looking to invest heavily in altcoins try Binance out.

SoFi Invest

You may have heard of SoFi before, especially if you have a student loan. The company got its start refinancing student loan debt but has since greatly expanded its horizons. SoFi now allows users to invest in many different products, including stocks, ETFs, and cryptocurrency.

In 2019, SoFi started to let its consumers trade crypto such as Bitcoin, Bitcoin Cash, Litecoin, and more. It has joined up with Coinbase to handle all crypto trades and transactions and, like we mentioned before, people really trust Coinbase. SoFi made the right choice with that partnership.

SoFi has been around for a while and has created a site that is accessible, easy, and is great for newcomers looking to invest for the first time. It is only available to US users, sadly, but anyone in America looking to get into crypto would be smart to check out SoFi.

Robinhood

You have likely heard of Robinhood because of their special and smart approach to investing. Through their trendy app and some major news stories, they have become one of the biggest names on Wall Street. It is undeniable that Robinhood has really become a game-changer in the stock market. They brought the ability to buy and trade stocks to the smartphone generation and game-ified trading. They made it feel fun and natural. Their impact on trading was immediate and important.

They got their start in stocks but Robinhood always had an eye on the world of cryptocurrency too. In February 2018, Robinhood started allowing its users to trade cryptocurrencies. That was a huge deal for crypto fans because Robinhood was already generating great interest among a young, new crowd looking to break into trading. Their inclusions of cryptocurrency only made that much more accessible for a new generation. Although some have protested some of Robinhood's methods and rules, most people admit that it has brought investing to a brand-new crowd in a way that just wasn't done before. They stand a good chance at creating a new wave of Bitcoin investors.

Robinhood started with only Ether and Bitcoin but gradually started to open up its choices of crypto and now has quite a few to choose from. It is all easy to do and just requires the simple tap of your finger on your smartphone. Some people say that investing with Robinhood is almost addictive, like a video game that makes you money.

Gemini

Gemini was started by Tyler and Cameron Winklevoss, the twin brother magnates who claimed they were the ones who created the idea for Facebook. Their foray into the cryptocurrency industry was met with a lot of speculation and attention, they had people ready to use Gemini before it even fully launched. To some it felt like a couple of tech start-up celebrities trying to climb aboard the next big thing without truly believing in it. Despite that, the Winklevoss twins put a lot of effort into Gemini to turn it into something special that could stand on its own.

They certainly created a strong, impressive product. Gemini is a fully regulated US Bitcoin exchange and is run with a lot of capital requirements and regulations that you would see at a standard bank. That brings a lot of peace of mind to some users because it feels as if there is a lot more security than you may find on other exchanges. The Winklevoss twins have crafted an online exchange that is easy to look at, easy to use, and feels secure, safe, and cutting-edge.

The look and feel of Gemini is slick and modern and looks very hip, it makes investing in crypto feel cool. The entire site is incredibly user-friendly too and has a lot of intuitive controls that will make it a breeze for first-time users. Great emphasis has been put on creating an easy and painless investing process.

Is it a perfect exchange? No, it isn't. While Gemini has a lot going for it, it also has a few downsides as well. Sadly, there aren’t a lot of currencies to choose from and the community is still so tight-knit that it may feel small to some. It is hard to make a lot of connections with others on the site. Others claim that Gemini feels sterile and cold, the opposite of inclusive. However, it works really well and looks great too and is accessible in many regions such as the US, Canada, Japan, the UK, and more.

What do you need to open an account?

Before you buy into your first cryptocurrency, you need to decide which route you will take. Which crypto exchange has earned your patronage? Once you have made up your mind with that, you need to sign up for an account and have your information ready. Keep in mind that you will be purchasing your first bitcoins with your debit or credit card so your banking information will need to be on hand.

Keep in mind that some banks do not like their customers investing in cryptocurrency. That is not surprising considering the whole idea behind crypto was to eliminate the need for banks. So, your bank may prevent you from allowing a deposit in a crypto exchange. A full-on block on investing is unlikely but you might need to check with your bank before you try to buy your first Bitcoin.

Depending on the exchange you settle on, you may need to provide personal documents such as a picture of your driver’s license, social security number, as well as your employer and source of income. Many people say that creating a crypto account is like creating a brokerage account. That makes sense because many people who like investing in stocks also like investing in crypto.

When you make your first investment, you need to be prepared for those exchange fees that will be charged. All the major exchanges demand a small fee. For example, Coinbase requires a 1.49% fee when using your bank account and a 3.99% fee when using a debit or credit card. The amount of the fee varies based on where you are purchasing your crypto so, again, you will want to research to find the avenue that fits best for you. Do not get charged too much!

If you decide to use one of those new-fangled Bitcoin ATMs, you will need the same information as listed above. You will also need to have your documents scanned by the ATM. It might be a little awkward to have all that stuff scanned in the middle of a mall or shopping center so be prepared for that process if you are using a Bitcoin ATM.

Once you have made your first purchase, you can now go straight to your wallet to watch your investment grow or start spending your Bitcoin. You have officially entered the crypto game and joined millions of other people who are buying, trading, and investing across the globe.

Bitcoin ATMS

Mostly everyone has experience using an ATM. If you have signed up for pretty much any bank in the world, you have likely had to insert your debit card into an ATM to deposit or withdraw cash. It has become one of the most commonplace and regular things that someone can do. But now the process of using an ATM has taken a step into the modern world with the creation of Bitcoin ATMs.

That's right, online exchange sites aren't the only way for you to dive headfirst into the world of Bitcoin investing. Investing has now become as easy as walking up to an ATM - literally. That is because there are now Bitcoin ATMs in the world. Just like a regular bank ATM, a Bitcoin ATM allows you to place your money in it in exchange for some Bitcoin. But since Bitcoin doesn't physically exist, placing your money in a Bitcoin ATM is like placing your fiat currency into the internet. You will never see it again, but you know that it is out there, making you money.

The concept behind Bitcoin ATMs is a little confusing because, unlike traditional ATMs, you are not depositing or withdrawing cash at one of these machines. Yes, you are placing cash into the machine. But it isn't going into your account. Instead, a Bitcoin ATM is used to buy and sell Bitcoin using fiat currency. So, you walk up to the machine, enter your information, deposit your cash, and then choose the sort of cryptocurrency you are looking to purchase. It is a way to enter the Bitcoin industry and invest without having to log into the computer.

What is the procedure for using a Bitcoin ATM, and how common are they? Let's look into the specifics of how they work and why they are growing in importance and popularity.

Buying or selling with a Bitcoin ATM is rather simple and resembles the steps taken at a regular, traditional ATM. First, you approach the machine and the first thing you have to do is verify your identity. Typically, most Bitcoin ATMs will ask you to enter your mobile phone information. After that is entered, you will be sent a text message to verify your identity before moving forward.

From there, you will be allowed to buy or sell your Bitcoin. If you are looking to purchase Bitcoin at an ATM, you must deposit the appropriate amount of cash. The machine will then generate a unique QR code that will be scanned by your digital wallet app on your phone. This way the ATM is telling your digital wallet that the transaction was a success and to add the digital funds to your wallet. Throughout the entire process, no money is ever changed hands aside from the initial deposit made into the machine.

Finally, if you are looking to sell Bitcoin via a Bitcoin ATM, you are going to use a QR code created by the Bitcoin ATM. Once the machine confirms that you are looking to sell some of your Bitcoin portion, the machine may feed you cash in exchange for the sold Bitcoin while other ATMs may take some time and you may need to come back to it to withdraw your cash.

The entire process is rather easy and seamless and has added a layer of comfort and normalcy to the entire process of buying or selling Bitcoin. The creation of Bitcoin ATMs was a huge step in making the idea behind cryptocurrency more normal and common and something that everyday investors could get behind. Everyone is familiar with ATMs, and everyone has used them a countless amount of times. The act of using an ATM is about as commonplace as using a seat belt. If more people can see that buying and selling Bitcoin doesn't have to be a complicated process that requires you to stay at home in front of your computer, it may go a long way to improve the popularity and mass appeal of Bitcoin.

Why should you use a Bitcoin ATM if you start investing in the cryptocurrency market? There are several reasons why this method of buying and selling is the right approach for you. Perhaps the biggest reason you should use a Bitcoin ATM is also the biggest reason why you should use a regular ATM: it is very convenient. Ideally, there will come a day when you see Bitcoin ATMs all over your city. Hopefully, they become as common as regular, traditional, fiat ATMs. If that becomes the case, using a Bitcoin ATM will become easy, practical, and a complete breeze.

When Bitcoin ATMs were first created, there were only a few of them across the country and all over the world. It took some time but the number available has multiplied substantially over the years. As of October 2020, it is estimated that there are over 9,000 ATM kiosks all over the United States. These ATMs are all owned by separate companies, and they have their own transaction fees tied to each use, so keep that in mind when you are making your buy or sell. You will have to spend a little extra money every time you use a Bitcoin ATM but the convenience and ease that it adds is worth it.

WHAT ARE ALTCOINS & WHICH SHOULD I INVEST IN?

We briefly mentioned altcoins before, but it is important you know a lot more about them as you enter the world of cryptocurrency investments. Why? Because altcoins have become one of the most powerful and important parts of the market. They are not just some lesser-than item that have no value. No, they are their own industry that is growing and, at times, thriving.

People who take the world of crypto investing seriously also take altcoins seriously. But they know which ones to look for, they know how they work, they know when to buy and when to sell. Like Bitcoin itself, there is much to know about altcoins. Luckily, the more you know, the better you will do. As with all things, knowledge is power.

If you thought investing in Bitcoin was overwhelming, just wait until you enter the world of altcoins. Keeping up with Bitcoin is difficult enough with its vast changes in the market and fluctuating value, there is a lot to keep in mind and a lot to remember as you traverse the ever-changing landscape of crypto. But the altcoin market is filled with literally thousands of choices. There are so many different coins you can invest in, some with great potential and some with none. Plus, it seems that the marketplace is always growing. Not a day goes by that investors do not hear of a new altcoin that may be the next big thing...or may be a total bust.

Trying to stay on top of all the selections is enough to drive anyone crazy. At the same time, truly understanding altcoins and how they work is an important part of being an informed and smart investor.

It is true that there are many, many altcoins to choose from. In fact, there are so many that it is impossible to remember them all. Thankfully, there are a few different choices that have risen through the ranks and are now the cream of the crop. These are the altcoins that have proven themselves more important and valuable than others. While there are obviously many, many more choices you could make, here are just a few that have again and again shown that they have what it takes to stand out among the very crowded crowd. However, it is important to remember that even these are the best of the best, there are plenty more you can find out in the online exchanges. This is truly just the tip of the iceberg, and you may find the next big altcoin craze where you least expect it. As with all things related to investing, be very careful.

Litecoin

Here is an altcoin you may be familiar with, even if you haven't done much research of your own. Why? Because Litecoin is perhaps the biggest name in the game and sometimes goes hand in hand with Bitcoin itself. Created in 2011, Litecoin has quickly become the gold standard of altcoins. There is no doubt or question that Litecoin is the top of the top and the highest-rated and most sought-after altcoin on the market. Its popularity and success has steadily gained and, most impressively, stayed stable throughout its lifetime.

Like all altcoins, Litecoin doesn't cost as much as Bitcoin. That is just one of the many benefits of it. You do not have to lose as much money when you are investing in Litecoin. But that is not the only feature that makes this altcoin so incredibly attractive to all types of investors.

Aside from being cheaper and faster than Bitcoin, there are a lot more Litecoins to mine. In fact, the altcoin was started as a branch of Bitcoin originally. It now has a supply limit of about 84 million which is far more than the limit Bitcoin will have. That means there is more of a supply of Litecoin, which means you have a better chance at buying cheaper because the demand doesn't affect supply as much as it does with other altcoins or especially with Bitcoin. That is why some people see Litecoin as the best altcoin to look into when you are first starting your investing career.

Ethereum

Whenever you mention Litecoin as the gold standard for altcoins, you have to mention Ethereum, which is definitely the silver to Litecoin’s gold. It may not be as popular as Litecoin but it still has a large, loyal fanbase that sings its praises often.

This altcoin has grown in popularity over the years and has shown how devoted it is to the future of all cryptocurrencies. In 2016, Ethereum split off into two: Ethereum and Ethereum Classic. The price of Ethereum is relatively low compared to Bitcoin and some other altcoins but many predict its value will only go up and up.

Perhaps the most promising aspect of Ethereum is its speed. While it can take around 15 minutes for a Bitcoin to be encrypted, mined and cleared, it only takes about 15 seconds for the same to be done with Ethereum. That isn’t just fast, it is blazingly fast.

The speed is great, the price isn’t as lovely. It will cost you a pretty penny to invest in Ethereum. But it has such promise and growth ahead that you would be silly to write off Ethereum. For serious investors, those who don't mind throwing down a large chunk of change on the promise of more, Ethereum is the way to go.

Bitcoin Cash

The name for this altcoin confuses many. Bitcoin Cash? So, this is a form of Bitcoin? If that is the case, why don't people just buy Bitcoin.

We understand the confusion and know an explanation is needed.

Bitcoin Cash is one of the earliest altcoins on the market and holds the distinction of being an oft-publicized hard fork from the original Bitcoin. A fork is when developers and miners cannot see eye-to-eye and the decision is made to create vast differences in the code and branch off and make a brand-new form of crypto.

When this happens, the original blockchain continues and the new cryptocurrency and its chain “fork” off and go their own way. That is what happened with Bitcoin Cash, or BCH. In August 2017, it was created and took off on its own. Since then, it has proven to be quite successful in its own right. However, its name and affiliation to Bitcoin has led to some confusion. Still, those in the know have long wanted a piece of Bitcoin Cash and that is one of the reasons why it has lasted so long.

Celsius

Back in 2018, the Celsius Network earned $20 million via crowdfunding. That shows just how interested people were in Celsius even before it officially launched. Since its creation and release, it has written more than $6 million in loans and has generated more than $11 million in interest alone.

The fact that Celsius has generated so much money in loans is both surprising, encouraging, and also cause for concern. We have seen numerous loaning giants fold over the years because of bad investing and loans that do not get paid back. It is possible that Celsius is just too new to the loaning world and will make some bad beds and offer loans to people who do not and should not get them. If that is the case, you can expect Celsius to face some serious problems in the years ahead. However, there is a chance that Celsius will only continue to expand instead of collapse.

Polkadot

Polkadot is an altcoin that was a huge hit right out of the gate. Upon its release, Polkadot’s value rocketed up 41% in just one week.

Polkadot was created by Gavin Woods, one of the guys behind Ethereum. One of its major appeals to investors is the fact that Polkadot uses parachains. A parachain is multiple blockchains all being used at once. This improves on the traditional concept and procedure of mining coins, it means you can do it all much faster. A faster mining process is better for all of those involved. Instead of one blockchain being mined, there are multiple chains being mined at the same time. Parallel chains mean more chances at success.

That is part of the reason why people were so keen on the idea of Polkadot upon its release. Its explosive debut was no surprise because of its superior technology and ultra-fast mining speed. You should always keep an eye on any altcoin that is changing the way things are done, just like Polkadot does.

Cardano

Charles Hoskinson co-founded Cardano after lots and lots of research from mathematicians and other experts in the field. He was originally a part of Ethereum but left to create his own crypto after disagreements inside that altcoin about how to run it.

In-depth and intricate research is what makes Cardano so special. The creators of the coin have written over 90 papers on blockchain technology and have repeatedly proven that they know what they are talking about and are experts on all things related to crypto and blockchain.

Because of their expertise with blockchain, Cardano has been leaving its competitors in the dust when it comes to computing and mining speed. That is a very important part of all types of coins, Bitcoin, and altcoins. The better the technology, the faster the mining, the easier it is to buy and the shorter the transaction times. This all adds up to Cardano being a very special, very fast, very cutting-edge form of altcoin.

These are just some of the altcoins you can invest in, of course. There are many, many more where they came from - and many more to come. It seems that more altcoins are being released regularly. In fact, there are now businesses that are creating their own altcoins, as a way to drum up business and attract a fanbase that is trendy and modern. Is this a going trend? Something we will see more of in the future? That is very likely. There may come a day when some of the biggest corporations in the world allow you to buy and trade and pay with their own forms of cryptocurrency or altcoins. That would be cutting edge and in tune with the modern world. Time will tell what the future holds for altcoins.

WHAT CAN YOU USE BITCOIN FOR?

If you have finally purchased Bitcoin, you may be asking yourself one major question: just what can I use it for?

That may sound like a dumb question but it actually isn't because not many people are very aware of what you can and can't use Bitcoin for. Some people do not understand the vast functionality Bitcoin has. Others are only putting their money into the market because they want to turn a profit, cash out, and then use fiat currency once they have amassed their fortune.

If you are looking to just make money off of your Bitcoin investment, then you know what you can use it for: just to create money. But if Bitcoin is more than just an investment and a way to fatten your portfolio, it makes sense that you want to know how to actually spend it in the real world. There are a lot of ways to use your Bitcoin, plenty of options and more and more coming regularly. The opportunities you have with your Bitcoin collection is always growing and has come a long, long way from where it was only a few years ago.

In the years since its creation, all cryptocurrencies including Bitcoin have only become more popular. You don't need to be a crypto expert to see that. Just reading through the business section of the newspaper regularly will give you headline after headline about the growing popularity of cryptocurrency. Though they may not have broken into the mainstream in some ways, mostly everyone is at least passingly familiar with the idea behind Bitcoin. They know it as a digital currency, something you never hold in your hands or place into your bank but somehow has some sort of value. It is also true that some people have needless biases and suspicions about Bitcoin. One of the biggest mistaken beliefs about it is that it can't be spent like normal money. Some people think that Bitcoin can only be used in a select few places online or in the real world. They think it is mostly useless. It is not.

Since cryptocurrency is exactly that - a currency - the idea is that you can use it to buy stuff. That is what currency is technically for, right? What sort of stuff can you acquire with Bitcoin? Lots of stuff actually and more and more all the time. Your options may be more limited than they are with a traditional credit or debit card, but you can use your cryptocurrency to purchase a whole lot of things. In the years since its creation, your ability to make common purchases with Bitcoin has exploded. There was a time when your choices were slim and most people were only using Bitcoin as a way to show how knowledgeable they were about the tech world. Now you can buy plane tickets, groceries, home décor, and a whole lot more with your hard-earned Bitcoin.

But before we get to that, we need to cover a very important topic when it comes to buying, selling, and storing Bitcoin. You need to know all about the Bitcoin wallet.

When you buy cryptocurrency, it is stored in something called a digital wallet. This is essentially like your bank account and it looks a lot like a smartphone app or website created by your bank to keep tabs on your checking account. No matter which digital wallet you end up purchasing, navigating through it will feel like second nature if you have any experience logging into your bank account online. The wallet is where you will be able to see the current price and value of your Bitcoin investment. However, this digital wallet also comes with keys. These randomly generated keys are essentially your money, they are the codes you will submit when you are buying your items or sending coins to someone else. Think of the keys as little debit cards. They are tied to your account and will move value from your wallet.

Purchasing a digital wallet isn't nearly as easy as buying a physical one you keep fiat currency in. That is because there isn't just one type of wallet. In fact, there are two types of digital wallets, and you need to decide which you want before you move forward.

The first type of digital wallet is called a hot wallet. A hot wallet is a wallet that is stored in the cloud, you access it via the internet. This is the sort of procedure that resembles your current online banking system. With a hot wallet, you simply log in and can see your current Bitcoin worth and can make transactions, sell, buy, and trade right there in the hot wallet app.

Most online exchanges will provide you with your very own hot wallet when you sign up and make your first investment. They have to, as a hot wallet is an elementary and essential part of assessing your Bitcoin value and knowing where your account stands. However, you can also find many different hot wallet options off of the site you purchase your crypto through. Sites such as Coinbase, Electrum, Blockchain, and more offer the ability to secure a hot wallet through their sites. Why would you want to do that instead of just staying with the wallet provided to you from your exchange? Because certain sites have insurance on their wallets and some do not, some sites have higher security and protective measures and some do not. You need to weigh your options when you are deciding on your hot wallet home.

The other type of wallet would be a cold wallet. Unlike the hot wallet, the cold really is a physical object that you can hold in your hands and place in your pockets and take on the go with you. The cold wallet is a portable device that allows you to download and carry your bitcoin wherever you are. They can cost a pretty penny, many of them sell for about $100, but they will keep your Bitcoin information securely stored at all times. The cold wallet takes your Bitcoin out of the cloud and downloads it straight to the wallet. It keeps it close to you at all times. For many, this is an extra security feature that adds great value. For others, it is too risky. You know the feeling you have when you lose your wallet and all the possessions inside? Imagine if you lost your cold wallet and all of your Bitcoin was gone forever. You can see why a cold wallet can be an asset and a detriment.

If you decide to invest in a cold wallet, you will have multiple options about where to buy them. More than anything, take careful time creating a password that is safe and strong. Treat your wallet as if it is one of the most important things in the world to you. If you play your cards right and invest well, it very well may be.

So now you have secured your investment, you have bought your wallet too. What's next? Well, buying something of course. Now it is time for you to put your hard-earned coin into practice and finally secure a purchase with your Bitcoin. Don't be afraid, it isn't nearly as hard as you may imagine. In fact, buying things with Bitcoin is sometimes considerably easier than buying something with fiat currency.

The process of buying something with bitcoin is easier than you would expect. Recently, many online retailers have made the checkout process incredibly intuitive and stress-free. It is as easy and painless as paying with a credit or debit card. At most websites that accept Bitcoin, you simply pay for your purchase just like you would with a credit or debit card. There are a few extra steps and it isn't an exact replica of using a card, but the process is very similar. Most people who pay for something via Bitcoin have remarked on just how easy and quick the entire process is.

The question is where can you shop with your cryptocurrency. There are a lot of options - and they are only growing. Currently, there are some pretty big eCommerce sites, such as Overstock, that allow you to shop with your Bitcoins. There are other sizable businesses that are now letting their customers purchase goods and services with cryptocurrency. Some online travel agencies are letting folks purchase plane tickets with crypto, while other sites related to the latest in technology and computers do too. You can even order a pizza and pay with your cryptocurrency. That is a very modern way of getting dinner.

But it isn’t just online purchases that can be completed with Bitcoin. As cryptocurrency becomes more popular, there are now in-person brick-and-mortar establishments that allow you to shop with your crypto investments. Thanks to certain payment processing systems like Square and Shopify, many small and local businesses now accept Bitcoin. That means you can support your local companies in a way that you never could have before. Plus, thanks to the new technology, paying with Bitcoin is easier than ever. It is as simple as taking out and swiping your wallet. As Bitcoin becomes more and more popular, more advances in the system will be made. But as it is now, paying with your Bitcoin wallet isn't that hard at all. For some, it is confusing to spend money without taking out an actual, physical wallet made of leather. But the digital wallet is fast, safe, effective, and immediate. That is one of the reasons why there are more and more retailers that are getting into the world of Bitcoin. They see that it is easy to set up and easy to use. It is a win-win for any business that invests in it.

Still, fans of Bitcoin are waiting for a major retailer along the lines of Target, Walmart, or Amazon to join in the crypto revolution and start accepting it at their locations. This has long been one of the biggest goals for crypto aficionados but has always remained just out of reach. There are many reasons why this dream has never come true and we will discuss those later in the book. But the fact remains that while Bitcoin is continually growing in popularity in many ways, it has failed to catch on in mainstream shopping in some pretty important ways. That could change in an instant. When it does, it will propel Bitcoin forward even more and will take it to the next level. Bitcoin proponents have long been crying out for some of these massive corporations to take a chance and allow their customers to pay with Bitcoin. While some companies, like Overstock, have listened most are a bit afraid to move forward and try their hand at cryptocurrency. Despite that, the technology marches on and buying, securing, and keeping tabs on your Bitcoin investment is now as easy as just logging into your digital wallet and seeing where things stand.

When Bitcoin first started, it was hard to find things you could buy with your crypto. However, times have changed and the list of buying options you will have once you make your first Bitcoin investment is only growing.

IN CONCLUSION

Buying Bitcoin isn't as easy as just putting your money into the system and deciding how much you want to buy. It is a lot more complicated than that. There is much to know, there are all sorts of particulars and concepts to keep in mind when you are buying. It is far more than just spending money. It is about understanding the market, it is about finding the right time to invest, it is about making a plan and sticking to it.

Now you know what it takes to make your first investment. Now you know the specifics that you need to keep in mind when you are looking to begin your Bitcoin career. With this knowledge in hand, you can move forward. But there is still more to know. If you want to be more than just a Bitcoin beginner, you need to know even more intricate details about how the system and market works.

5

INVESTING IN CRYPTOCURRENCY FOR BEGINNERS

You are well acquainted with the facts about investing in Bitcoin. You know a bit about the market now. You know certain online exchanges you can use to make your first investment. You know what to expect, volatility and all, from the world of cryptocurrency. Are you an expert? Maybe not, but you know enough to get into the game and finally make your first investment. The world is your oyster and Bitcoin offers one giant oyster if done correctly.

But there is more to know. There is a lot more to know. The best investors will tell you that buying into a market isn't as simple as just putting your money into an account and making a purchase. If that's your approach to Bitcoin, good luck. You may in fact end up making money and watching your fortune grow. But the chances of you making a really impressive sum by just following the basic steps verbatim are slim.

You stand a much better chance at success in the world of Bitcoin if you truly understand the market. What does that mean? How does someone understand a market? What sort of work does that take? What does that even look like?

It is a major mistake to look at a market as just a place where trades and buys happen. To do that totally ignores some of the most important nuances and subtleties that you need to comprehend if you want to make it in the world of Bitcoin.

Think of it this way: the market is a living and breathing thing. It has moods, it has emotions, it has good days and bad days. Some things upset it more than others, while some things make it really happy. Like a real person, a market has an entire personality that you can learn about and eventually understand. Once you know how the market works, how it feels, you will be able to predict its reactions, movements, and moods a whole lot better.

The market doesn't just exist in a vacuum, either. It is a part of a larger economy and is affected by a whole bunch of things that you might not think have an impact on it. If you ignore these outside events, you lose the chance to create smart, financially sound, and profitable investments.

The goal of investing is to understand the market you are in, warts and all. The goal is to also understand the world around the market and the external events that will heavily influence and impact the market. If you look at the market as a cold, lifeless, and clinical object that doesn't react like a living creature, you are bound to make mistakes and miss multiple chances to enhance your profits and make a name for yourself.

Understanding the market is the best piece of advice you can be given before you start your investing journey. And how do you do that? Let's start by taking a good, deep look at how exactly the market works.

HOW CRYPTOCURRENCY MARKETS WORK

It is time for us to go back to school. Don't worry, we aren't learning about chemistry or language arts. Instead, we are learning all about the cryptocurrency market. That's right, we need to start a little Market 101 class. It is important to have a good handle on how and why crypto works and what affects the values of coins. Once you have a better understanding of that, you will find that trading becomes just a bit easier. This is a pivotal part of understanding the market and investing wisely.

So, here is a question that is worth a million dollars - and a million Bitcoins too: what drives the market? What are the main factors behind rising and falling prices, values, and profits? What should you look out for when you are trying to improve your standing in the crypto market?

You won't be surprised to learn that one of the major components driving the market is one that drives almost every market: supply and demand. Supply and demand is a term you have heard throughout your life and especially in any classes related to economics, it is something that most people know about. But how many people truly understand it? It is a factor that plays a massive deal in the value of Bitcoin and other cryptocurrencies. Supply and demand is the primary force behind the fluctuations in the crypto market. As we previously discussed, unlike other trading venues, the cryptocurrency market is decentralized and remains free of the constraint as well as the watchful eyes of many political and financial institutions. It is solely powered by the will and interest of the people involved. The more people that want it, the better the value of Bitcoin. It is as simple as that.

Many see crypto trading as the Wild West of markets because of its lack of regulations. While it means that you will not be slowed down by many rules and strict enforcements that are followed on Wall Street, it also means that there is more of a risk. You are not looked after as much as you are when trading traditional stocks, at least not by a major and heavily-financed governmental agency. The crypto market can change suddenly because of supply and demand and there will not be as many precautions put into place to rescue you if things go south. You need to be extra careful - and extra confident - when trading in crypto. Simply put, if things go in the wrong direction and you make a great fall, there is no net below to catch you. The lack of regulations can be both a good and a bad thing.

As stated, supply and demand is the biggest engine driving the major changes in the cryptocurrency market. The number of coins in circulation and the desire of others to get their hands on them is really what spikes the price of the coins. That is an easy concept to wrap your head around and keep in mind as you start your trading career. What other factors play into the value of crypto? There are a few.

WHAT AFFECTS THE MARKET

Outside Events

The crypto market isn’t immune to scandal and security breaches and general economic uncertainty. Any of those things can cause people to buy or sell their coins and can affect the market in some major ways. If the global economy hits a rough patch, you may see the value of crypto plummet as people sell what they have to secure currency that feels more “real” or easier to hold onto in a traditional bank. If there is a major scandal related to crypto or a security breach that shows its weaknesses, you may see people fleeing from crypto for a while for the same reasons. When people get scared, they sell. When they sell, the value goes down.

Market integration

This is a major factor when you are talking about the value of cryptocurrencies. As cryptocurrencies grow in popularity, many mainstream businesses and markets want to be involved with them. Over the last few years, there have been instances of crypto integration in some major ways. Certain businesses have started to accept crypto as a form of payment, many websites have started to as well. When this happens more and more, it will only make more people interested in getting a piece of crypto and that will drive up the value of your investment. Make sure to keep an eye on the latest developments in the world of cryptocurrency and try to have an understanding of the business side of things and how well crypto is expanding in the world.

The Press

The media has always been wary of Bitcoin and other cryptocurrencies. Ever since the inception of Bitcoin, many in the press have not known how to handle or report on crypto. To this day, their stories are sometimes slanted or confused and can paint cryptocurrencies in a negative light. These news stories could damage the reputation of crypto and could lead to a lack of demand or a sell-off of certain coins. Crypto is not immune to nasty headlines.

As you can see, there are a few components that play into the current value of cryptocurrencies and coins on the market. When you start buying and trading crypto, you need to make yourself aware of these things. Perhaps check the news more often, be alert of any breaking crypto developments. It cannot hurt to invest in a subscription to a well-respected business newsletter or blog. Watch how Bitcoin and any Bitcoin-related developments are handled in the media. For every major event, check the value of your coins and see if it has fluctuated at all. See if you can note a correlation between the world at large and your investment. If you are tuned into the general state of cryptocurrencies, you will become a better trader and you will be making moves that are safer and healthier in the long run. The worst thing you can do is go into crypto trading blind and without any knowledge of the many facets impacting it. Make sure you remember that the market is a living, breathing thing that has reactions that often feel emotional. Try to figure out those emotions and you will be better prepared for the next cycle.

Why Trade Crypto?

Why trade cryptocurrency? Perhaps the bigger question is why not trade cryptocurrency. The reasons for wanting to trade cryptocurrency are vast and we have covered many of them already. When you look at the history of Bitcoin, you can see exactly why you would want to invest in and trade crypto. It started at a value less than a single penny, then the price of a couple pizzas, and has now grown into a commodity that is worth thousands upon thousands of dollars. Think of how much money you would have made if you had gotten in on the ground floor, invested wisely, and traded when you needed to.

But there is no use looking back on the past and wishing you had become involved years ago. The truth is that there is still great promise and potential in Bitcoin today, right now. You do not need a time machine to find yourself in the midst of great growth and worth all thanks to Bitcoin.

There is so much money to be made in cryptocurrency. In fact, there is more money to be made currently than there ever has been. While the market is often volatile and unpredictable, its peaks have proven to be much larger than its valleys. Additionally, the volatility of the crypto market can be a benefit for your investment because, for every downturn, there can be just as big of an upturn too. Just make sure you are strapped in and buckled up for both the good and the bad - because there will definitely be both and a smart investor is aware - and ready - for them both.

Some people are drawn to cryptocurrencies precisely because of that wild unpredictability. Others are frightened by this aspect of it. Either way, trading crypto attracts a lot of attention.

Why should you trade crypto? There are several reasons but let’s look at the biggest ones.

Lack of restrictions: Unlike many brokerage firms on Wall Street, there aren’t nearly as many rules and regulations that prevent you from doing what you want when trading crypto. That does pose a few extra risks but the freedom that it grants is enough to bring a lot of people on board. Some people want complete control of their financial decisions, regardless of the consequences. There is something to be said for that, but you need to be very cautious when investing in cryptocurrency. Remember that fewer rules mean more risk.

Market hours: when you trade stocks on Wall Street, you have to follow Wall Street’s hours. That doesn’t apply to trading in the cryptocurrency market. Since there is no centralized body governing crypto, you can trade 24 hours a day. You are not going through a third party when you are buying or selling your coins, you are working directly with an individual. This is a huge bonus for many traders.

Variety: though most people talk about Bitcoin when they are referring to cryptocurrency, there are hundreds upon hundreds of different coins to invest in. As we discussed before, these altcoins are vast, varied, and stand a chance to explode seemingly overnight. A smart investor can research and find a variety of coins to buy in and, if done correctly, they can make a fortune by finding up-and-coming altcoins.

WHERE CAN YOU TRADE CRYPTOCURRENCY?

Much like stocks, there are plenty of places for you to spend your money and buy and trade your cryptocurrencies. Over the years, the amount of locations that deal with trading has only grown and now you have quite a large selection to choose from.

Investing or trading in Bitcoin can be very intimidating, especially if you haven’t invested in anything else before. It feels like there are many stories on the internet about scams and frauds ripping people off. It is true, there are scammers out there who would love nothing more than to part you from your cash. However, if you go to a reputable exchange, you stand no risk of losing your money in such a way. Many of these exchanges put a lot of focus on safety and privacy.

Perhaps the biggest name in crypto exchange is Coinbase. Coinbase was founded in 2012, right as Bitcoin was taking off. It has massively grown since then and now has over 20 million users and supports 14 different types of cryptocurrencies. Of course, you can buy and trade Bitcoin at Coinbase, but you can also invest in Litecoin, Ethereum, and many more too. Plus, Coinbase will give you a $5 bonus when you sign up with them. That is not a bad deal.

The mobile trading app Robinhood has gotten into the crypto game too and you can buy and trade Bitcoin and others with their service. Robinhood is often controversial for multiple reasons, some of them completely valid, but it cannot be denied that they have brought the power of trading to the common person and have made investing easier and even a bit fun for many people. Robinhood is a great approach to trading, not only with cryptocurrencies but with other traditional stocks too. Robinhood also sweetens the pot by giving you a free share of stock when you open an account with them.

TradeStation is another online exchange that specializes in cryptocurrency for casual traders. They have created a site and system that is easy to use and comes with $0 stock and ETF trades. They are a great destination for new traders or more advanced ones too.

Bittrex should be your approach to trading crypto if you are looking to invest in more altcoins. Bittrex focuses heavily on altcoins and has over 100 coins you can invest in. That is quite the variety, especially since so many lesser-known altcoins are harder to come by with many online exchanges.

The approach to buying a coin at all of these locations is very similar. You will need an account at each of these locations and will need to fund your account with your bank account or credit card. From there, the world will be your oyster and you will be able to find the coins you want to buy and trade.

HOW TO TRADE CRYPTOCURRENCY

So, you have purchased your first taste of cryptocurrency and now the question is how do you trade if you want to? Trading is a major aspect of investing in cryptocurrency and is one of the biggest parts of the entire process for many people.

Some people want to invest in crypto solely so they can start trading. It can be a very exciting way to make money and watch the market. Some people want to ride the waves of the crypto market and trade when the timing is right in order to make more money.

Some other people do not want anything like that, however. They would much rather just make their initial investment and let it sit and possibly accrue more value over time. But these people do not always want to rock the boat and attempt to trade and make more money for fear of losing out on the investment they initially made. Due to the volatility of the market, attempting to always find the greener grass on the other side could cause you to lose the worth of your investment originally had.

But let’s assume you want to do a fair amount of trading once you have entered the crypto market. You need to be prepared and very aware of how the system works so you can make the right choices and do it in the right amount of time. Any good investor will tell you that timing really is everything when it comes to trading. If you do it too soon, you miss out. If you do it too late, you always miss out.

So how does it work? What do you need to know when you are making your crypto trades?

It depends on what avenue you are going through. If you are using a site to make your trades, the entire system is pretty seamless and easy. But before you make your trades, you need to be planning ahead and know what you are trading for. Are you exchanging some of your Bitcoins for a smaller, lesser-known altcoin? All of this information will be asked of you, so you need to have your information intact and ready to go.

Thankfully, there is one aspect of the crypto market that is not present in Wall Street and the regular stock market: it is open 24/7. That is a huge bonus because you can do a lot of your research and market watching at night. However, that does not mean every time of the day will be highly profitable and successful for you. There will be long stretches of the day when nothing is really happening in the market. Be prepared for many parts of the day to be a bit uneventful.

You also need to be prepared for a lot of options and a lot of different coins trying to get your attention. The fear of missing out can be a trader’s worst enemy. It always feels like the best is yet to come and that may lead to you instantly having regret about a trade you made. It may also lead to you wanting to trade again. And again. And again.

The best option is to really study the market, which can be done via whichever online exchange you sign up for and put your money into. Take your time, truly examine the ins and outs of the crypto world and start to craft a strategy that is right for you and only you. Do not let your emotions get the best of you and do not jump on a bandwagon because it seems like the most popular route. Just because something is popular doesn’t mean it will be right for you. Do not cave to what others are doing. Try to strike out on your path and create a system that works for you.

If you are doing an atomic swap, which is a peer-to-peer trade between two individuals free of a third party, you need to have two things: your wallet and trust. You will need your digital wallet ready because the information embedded within it is necessary when you are trading your coin. Make sure you have that all prepared before you embark on any atomic swap. Trust is probably just as important as your wallet because these trades are done without any supervision, no one will stop you from making a mistake. Whatever choice you make will be permanent. The idea of no oversight or regulation is what attracts many people to crypto trading, but it can also be dangerous. Be very, very careful with the relationships you build and the trades you make.

Here is the biggest question you should ask when trading crypto, no matter which way you do it: why? Why are you looking to make a trade and why are you looking to make the trade you are contemplating? Is it simply because you are bored with the crypto you currently hold? If that is the case, stop what you are doing and give yourself a few days to think things through. Like we said earlier, do not just cave to passing speculation and wanderlust.

Now, if you have done a lot of research and think you are making the right trade, that is a different story and means that the trade you’re thinking about is worth your time. But what is the research you’ve done? Where did you get your information? What is it saying? When you start trading cryptocurrency, you need to become something of an expert on the market. You will need to find the right sources that give you the news and information you can truly use in your quest for trades. Once you find reliable sources that you feel comfortable with, it is only then that you should entertain the idea of trades.

The biggest mistakes people make when trading crypto is being impulsive and rash. You should not act too quickly; you should always try to take some time to consider the choices you’re making and any possible outcomes that will arise from them. At the same time, being too slow is also a dangerous approach to trading. That means you could miss out on some major market moves. The best approach is somewhere in the middle but that isn’t always easy to achieve.

Like all things related to cryptocurrency, do not expect to be a master trader right away. In fact, you should be ready for this process to feel confusing and overwhelming at first. You should also be prepared for it to take a long time before you feel very comfortable trading your cryptocurrencies. The best investors and traders did not achieve that status overnight. It takes a long time and has a steep learning curve. Be prepared for that and get rid of any hopes and dreams for a quick return on your investments.

The best advice you can get when you start trading in crypto is to watch the market. But do not just passingly watch it, you need to truly examine and understand the market. How does that work? How do you understand a market as complicated and volatile as the cryptocurrency market? Invest in some newsletters, sign up for email chains, subscribe to threads on Reddit or create a social media account on Twitter that focuses on crypto news. Find sources you trust online and read them regularly. The goal is to get to a point where you know why the market is behaving the way it is. You do not want to be asking how the market is, you want to be asking why the market is. Once you are at a place where you can see the moves the market makes before they happen, you are in a place that can allow for profitable and smart trades.

Once again, you are asking yourself why? Why should you trade at all if there are so many things to learn and so many potential pitfalls and dangers lurking? Simply because crypto trading can be very profitable for you and the gains you can make are enormous and impressive. But it does take work and a plan. You need to think things through and create a sort of trading agenda that works for you. Proper crypto trading is about figuring out what sort of trader you are. Are you adventurous? Cautious? You need to ask these questions and find all the answers before you can readily trade with confidence.

WHEN TO BUY BITCOIN

Here is the big question, the thing that plagues most people and confuses them as well. You have decided on which coin you want to buy, whether it is Bitcoin or an altcoin. You have found the online exchange you want to use, you have opened an account and turned in your documentation and even made your first investment.

Then the time has come for you to create your first cryptocurrency investment. You buy your portion of Bitcoin, or altcoin. You have made your first buy, you are now part of the global success story known as Bitcoin. Congratulations! Now you just sit back and watch your investment grow or buy and sell and trade in the world of cryptocurrency. Welcome to the future.

But it isn't that easy, is it? It isn't as simple as just buying what you want and watching its value grow and using it to make purchases and live your life. No, it isn't that easy at all. In fact, many people may grow frustrated and angry at the rate of growth in the world of cryptocurrency. Some people may feel flustered, ripped off, as if the entire thing is just a giant scam. Some people get really turned off buying Bitcoin or altcoins because they don't get the results they want and they find themselves feeling confused, lost, and left behind. There is nothing more defeating than plans for a success in Bitcoin being dashed.

Why do people fail when they jump into Bitcoin? Why do their investments never grow? Why do they never amass enough cryptocurrency to make their lives easier and make their purchases? Where do things typically go wrong for new investors?

Generally, failure in the world of cryptocurrency revolves around people not being sure when to buy their cryptocurrency. People can learn how to do it, that isn't that complicated as we have shown. But knowing how to buy cryptocurrency means nothing if you don't know when to buy it.

Even if you aren't looking to make a fortune off of Bitcoin, you should know the right time to make your investment. Some people are only looking to enter the Bitcoin field because they want to examine and ride the market like they would the stock market on Wall Street. Others want to use Bitcoin because they truly believe in it and want it to be the newest, most unique, and important way to pay for things and live their lives. Whatever the reason, it cannot hurt to get in at the right time, make your buy when things are perfectly lined up, and follow a few simple rules to keep your Bitcoin portfolio strong.

So, the question is not if you should buy Bitcoin or how you should buy Bitcoin. We have discussed that and know the answers. Here are the more important questions about buying Bitcoin. Here is the stuff you should remember when you are looking to invest.

Don't Try to Time the Market

While you may spend weeks or months or even years learning about the ins and outs of the Bitcoin market and trying to figure out just how it works, the truth of the matter is you will never be an expert on the precise turns that the market will take. In fact, no one will be. Even people who live and breathe Bitcoin cannot always accurately predict what is ahead. The volatility of the Bitcoin market is one of the most reliable things about it. That is, you can rely on its unreliability.

Some people don't see it that way. Some people are confident in their ability to watch the market and know the exact perfect time to jump into the game. They think they can find the right moment when the price of Bitcoin is at its lowest, right before it starts to gain value again. These people will wait and wait and wait, hoping that they will find the ideal period to make their investment. They will buy low, as low as it can get, they think. Then the value will just grow and grow and soon they will be making more money than they know what to do with.

This isn't the right move when you are buying Bitcoin. Far too many people will wait until doomsday comes if they are truly waiting for the right price to buy.

Honestly, there is always going to be a chance that the price of Bitcoin will drop lower than what it was at when you made your buy. You need to be comfortable with the fact that the situation could get cheaper, and you may not buy at technically the best time. Do not let that stress you out. Instead, you should be honest with yourself and lay out a price range that you feel good about. Choose a price that you want to buy at and stick to it! Make your buy at the price you set for yourself and don't look back. If it goes lower after you purchase, it goes lower. That is okay because you made your purchase at a point that you really thought about and felt good with.

Too many people wait for the precise moment when all the stars align, and the price of Bitcoin is at its lowest. First of all, you just never know when that will happen. Secondly, it isn't about getting when it it’s at its lowest, it's about getting in when you feel best about it. Do not hold your plans hostage because you are waiting for a dreamlike price point that may never come or may not be that helpful at all.

Perhaps one of the best pieces of advice a new investor can take - aside from don't wait and wait and wait - is the idea of not buying "in bulk." Far too many people decide they want to buy $2,000 worth of Bitcoin. They want to put all that cash down at one time and make a sizable investment in cryptocurrency. But why? Why place all your money into the market at one time. Why not spread out your investment over time? Buy $500 at a time, over time, until your entire sum of $2,000 has been invested. You don't need to spend all your money at one point. In fact, maybe you shouldn't. If you spend your money over time, it is easier on your heart and mind and doesn't feel as scary. Plus, you can save some money if the market takes a huge nosedive.

No matter when you buy or at what interval you invest, make sure you only spend what you are comfortable with. You should never spend more than you have, never spend more than you need. You should be able to keep living your life normally without losing it all in the Bitcoin market. Be smart or else you could end up with nothing at all.

Don't Forget Your Plan

Success can do some crazy things to people.

You may know or have heard of some investors who strike it big in Bitcoin. Perhaps they put a few hundred or thousand dollars into the market and then watched it skyrocket in a short amount of time. Boom, suddenly they have way more money than they started with.

That's a great deal, wonderful news for any investor. Hopefully, that happens to you too, if you plan to make money on Bitcoin. However, if it does, do not lose sight of the plan you laid out before things got started.

One of the biggest mistakes people can make when investing in Bitcoin relates to success. Success in the market can change someone and lead them down some silly paths that are full of roadblocks, mistakes, and pitfalls. This can all be avoided if you remember one thing: your plan.

Here's the way a plan works. Say you want to make double the amount you invest. That is a hefty goal, something that not everyone achieves. But that is what you are aiming for, your best-case scenario. You understand that you may not meet that goal, you may not double your investment. And you know that is okay, you are comfortable spending the money you are putting down and you are also comfortable with the fact that you may not get it back.

You enter the marketplace with that double-your-earnings goal and, as luck has it, your dreams actually come true. You find major profit and you've doubled your initial investment in a small amount of time. Great! Now what?

Now, you've made your goal and you should hold onto it, of course. You have reached your objective and you should sell. That seems counterintuitive to some. Many people say that you should stay in if the market is hot, you should keep your money invested. Heck, some say you should even invest more.

But that wasn't the plan. The plan was to double your investment. Once you have completed your plan, you need to stick to it. You need to stay smart. You need to stay responsible. There is a chance that the market could keep going up, you could potentially make even more than just double your earnings. That is a chance, and you have to live with that. However, the plan you created when you first invested was about making the ideal profit that you wanted. When you have achieved that, you need to be careful because, while you could make more, you could also lose it all.

Plans are important because they guide you in the right direction of what you want to accomplish. If you are looking to make money off of Bitcoin and are not just entering the market because you want to use this new form of currency fiat money, then you need to have a realistic and honest view of how much you want to make. Do you want to double your investment? Do you want to only add a few hundred dollars to it? Do you want to triple it? Do you want even more? Be honest about what you want, set your goal, and be truthful to yourself. You don't have to hide the fact that, yes, maybe you want to make a lot of money. That is okay, there is no shame in that.

However, once you meet your goal - whatever it is - you need to stay smart. If you don't, the success will go to your head and you will keep riding the market, thinking the good times will never end. But, the truth is that they always end and that could result in you having little to nothing. Instead of doubling your investment, you could end up losing it all.

Don't Panic

Overreacting or freaking out is never a good idea, that is true no matter what the situation is. Whether you are talking about buying a car, making an investment in any market, starting a new job, or deciding to move, you never want to make a rash and sudden overreaction to something.

The truth of the matter is that things are going to go bad every once in a while, in every facet of your life. If your reaction is over the top and simply reactionary, then you are only going to make things worse - sometimes a lot worse. Instead, you need to decide what your bottom line is, you need to know when you should really pull out of the market, you should be prepared for bad times and stick to a decision to not panic when things go from good to bad.

As has been mentioned multiple times, the cryptocurrency market is full of wild volatility. Sometimes it feels like there are just as many bad days as there are good days. There are a lot of ups and downs when you are investing in Bitcoin, a lot of peaks and valleys. The investor who is not ready for that will be blindsided when those bad times come, and their reaction may completely spoil any future potential they have.

This advice directly ties to the advice of making a plan and sticking to it. Part of your plan should take the dips in the market into consideration. Part of your plan needs to be about being prepared for any losses and drops in your Bitcoin's worth. You know it's coming; you know there will be a point when your market worth is not a number you're happy with. So how do you respond?

There are multiple ways to respond to a rough path in investing. Some people strap in and prepare for a stretch of bad times, knowing that they are going to ride it out no matter what. Some people make the opposite choice and decide that it is time to cut and run. Then there are the other types of investors: the ones who have a bottom line and know that they aren't pulling their money out until the value of your investment reaches that low point.

There is a benefit and disadvantage to all of those approaches. It is up to you which avenue you want to go down. Obviously, you want to make the most money possible, you don't want to end your investment and pull out your cash. Otherwise, you would have never invested in the first place. However, there is a time when maybe it makes sense to pull back. It is okay to admit that, sometimes the market can just feel too up and down and rough, sometimes it feels like you don't want to be a part of it for the future of your finances and the future of your sanity too. Losing money in the market can be a very traumatic experience and can deal a lot of psychological damage to you. Therefore, it is understandable that you may want to pull your money out if you are going through a rough patch that just feels too monumental in the moment.

That is the benefit of pulling your investment out when things go bad: you won't lose any more money. A lot of people will tell you to stick it out, ride out the bad times, don't pull out when things hit a low. That is good advice for most people but not for all people. If part of your plan is losing as little as possible, you should be realistic about pulling your cash out if things are getting too rough. There is no shame in ending something if it doesn't seem to be working out for you. While it is smart to push yourself and try to do new things, you should never do something that makes you feel completely uncomfortable.

The disadvantage of pulling out your money in a down period, of course, is that there is no chance to recoup your losses and make more money in the future. When you withdraw your investment and recede from the market, you are losing your chance to make any gains that could be just around the corner. As mentioned, for every down in the Bitcoin market, there is an up. If you happen to withdraw your cash during a down phase and then see an up phase follow shortly thereafter, you will probably feel pretty bad. So, you need to weigh your options and decide which feels worse: losing the money or missing an opportunity to make more money?

Depending on which of those choices feels worse to you, you should make the choice to either stay in or pull out. And if you decide to stay in and ride through the good and bad times, there is both advantage and disadvantage to that too. This may be an incredibly hard choice, even harder than withdrawing your investment. Why is it so hard? Because there are times when you don't see an end to the major nadirs in the cryptocurrency market. It feels like it could go on and on forever and there may never be light at the end of the tunnel. So, it can be very difficult to ride it out, even as you hold onto hope that the bad times will turn good again. Every day can be a battle and when you aren't seeing much progress, it can be very hard on you emotionally and mentally.

That is a major disadvantage, and you need to be comfortable with it when you are making that choice. The advantage is major, of course. The advantage would be that there will come a day when the market rebounds and you start making money again. It may not be tomorrow, it may not be next week or even next month, but it will happen. The bad times will not stay bad forever and if you are able to ride it out and keep enough money in the market to leave your head above water, you will more than likely make it to the other side. However, this advantage requires that you have a decent amount of money invested, which isn't an option for everyone. You also need to be comfortable with long dry spells, something that most people are not at all okay with. You must also keep in mind that, while you may soon experience another peak in the market, it might not ever reach the heights it once did. Yes, you will likely make it through the dark times but how bright will the bright times be? You need to keep in mind that you may never hit the highs you once did. If you aren't ready for that, you should probably not just ride the market through thick and thin.

That leaves the third and smartest option: creating a bottom line and sticking to it. This is very similar to creating your investing plan, something that was mentioned before. This option requires you to be very honest with yourself and your goals and create a number that is your rock bottom. You need to tell yourself that you will go no lower than this number and you need to stick to it. No matter what, come hell or high water, you need to withdraw your investment if your value drops below this number.

This is a smart and great choice for many new investors and also had an advantage and disadvantage too. The advantage here is that you will not lose too much money, you will be able to sleep at night knowing you have stuck to your initial plan. The disadvantage is that losing money is never easy and even though you know you have an eject button that will get you out of too much trouble, watching your value and worth dwindle away over time is not a good time. It will be hard for you to not want to pull out easy. It may also be hard to not allow yourself to lose more money than you agreed to. You may drop below that bottom line but think that maybe, just maybe, the market is about to turn around. It might, that is true. The market could rebound the day after your value drops below your rock bottom. But that doesn't matter because this plan requires that you create that bottom line number and stick to it. No matter what, you need to follow your rules here or else this entire plan doesn't work, and you are just throwing good money after bad.

In Conclusion

Investing in Bitcoin is a lot of work, and the work isn't only related to deciding how much money you want to put into the market. No, some of the hardest work related to Bitcoin investing is putting the time and effort and energy into understanding the market as a whole.

You really do need to understand how things work in the crypto market. You need to look at it like a living, breathing, reactionary creature that has feelings, emotions, moods, and a rather fickle personality. You need to comprehend this before you make your first investment. You also need to create a plan, a way to navigate the tricky cryptocurrency landscape and rules that you will follow when good times turn bad. You need to be a realist, a realist who knows when and why to pull your money out. If you are able to secure a plan for yourself and study the market like a scholar would, you could create a promising and financially sound future in the crypto market.

However, if things don't work out well, you could always become a miner. No, not the type of miner that digs in a cave. This type of miner is wildly different.

6

WHAT IS CRYPTO MINING AND HOW DOES IT WORK?

Let's say that things are going well with your first cryptocurrency investment. Or maybe they aren't, maybe it is a lot harder than you imagined it would be and you are not making the money you expected.

Do not panic and do not think that all of cryptocurrency is about investing. There is another way that people enter the cryptocurrency market, and it has nothing to do with putting their money into Bitcoin. In fact, there is a whole other side of Bitcoin that is all technical, all advanced and, for some, all successful.

Crypto mining is one of the most lucrative, expensive, intense, and complicated aspects of Bitcoin and it is something that has been thriving for years. Many people have devoted their entire lives to crypto mining. With good reason too, there is a lot of money in it. You could make literally thousands upon thousands of dollars in crypto mining, if you do it right and have a whole lot of luck. Yes, crypto mining requires a lot of skill, a lot of time and a lot of luck too.

But before we discuss how it works, we need to discuss what it is.

Crypto mining is probably a term you have heard many times if you are researching cryptocurrencies and looking to get into buying and trading. Indeed, mining is one of the most common things spoken about when people refer to crypto. It likely makes you think of an old gold miner, using a pickaxe to carve out some gold or diamonds deep in a dark and dangerous cave.

Sure enough, mining can help you find some diamonds of your own in the form of Bitcoin. It can indeed put a lot of cryptocurrency in your pocket. And like the miners from the Gold Rush of the 1800s, mining can also have you walking away with nothing at all. There is indeed great reward related to mining. There is also great risk.

So, what is it? Just what is this mining thing that so many people are drawn to? Even among seasoned cryptocurrency fans, mining is a confusing sticking point that raises more questions than answers. People who have long studied crypto still do not attempt to get into mining because of how daunting and challenging it is. What makes it so hard? Also, is it worth it? Is it more hard work than it is worth or is it a legitimate way to make yourself some extra money on the side?

Crypto mining is complicated. Not only is the actual process itself complicated, so is the explanation of it. We will try to break it down as easily and plainly as possible but do not be alarmed if it doesn’t make total sense.

Crypto mining is a very slow-going, painstaking process that essentially checks on the latest crypto transactions to verify them and make sure they are legitimate. The goal of mining is to avoid the dreaded idea of double-spending. Double-spending is something that specifically affects cryptocurrencies. It is when someone spends the same money in two places at once.

Double-spending isn’t possible with physical money. If you walk into a store and spend $10 on a sandwich, you hand over the $10 bill to the employee and it is no longer yours and you cannot then go spend it elsewhere. The same is true if you are paying with a debit or credit card. Whichever financial institution you are a part of will deduct the exact amount from your checking or credit card account immediately and that money is no longer yours to spend.

But when you are paying with crypto, it is theoretically possible for you to spend the money in two places at once. You are paying with codes, specific keys made for each transaction. And you are doing all of this without the regular checks and balances put in place by banks and other regular monetary systems. Cheating is possible if no one is looking.

That is where crypto mining comes into play. Mining is those checks and balances that you need to make sure no one is being scammed. However, mining isn’t done by an employee at your bank or an anonymous computer following a built-in procedure. Instead, mining is done by the very same people who invest in and trade crypto in the first place.

Cryptocurrency mining is a process that validates bitcoin transactions by adding them to the ledger or record of the currency, known as a blockchain. This is not an easy process. It is also not a short process. Mining is not something you can casually pick up or run off of just any computer.

Miners use specialty hardware to complete the complex calculations that will confirm Bitcoin transactions. In simple terms, what miners are doing is trying to assemble a massive and ultra-hard puzzle. There are thousands of miners working all at once, all trying to crack a code that has been sent to them. They are anonymous auditors all working towards the same goal. And whoever gets it first receives a prize: Bitcoins.

Mining continues the blockchain we spoke of before. Mining is the process of taking important information, like all the bitcoin transactions that occurred within the last 10 minutes, and verify them. From there, a computational puzzle must be figured out by a computer, also known as the miner.
