Major and popular companies or businesses that adopt crypto payments can also increase the value of cryptocurrencies across the board. That can be a restaurant chain, a hotel, or online retailers. Their inclusion of crypto payment would help legitimize cryptocurrency for customers.

Trading Platforms

Then, there are the platforms that offer traditional assets such as stocks or forex. If they announce that they would also deal in crypto trading, this would promote cryptocurrencies to their users.

Crypto Economy

The above are factors outside the cryptocurrency sphere. In the crypto world, several things can alter the price of a certain cryptocurrency. That could be a successful cyberattack, a new cryptocurrency that positions itself as a direct competitor to the coin you are investing in, etc.

In short, many things influence the price of a cryptocurrency, most of which are outside your control. Therefore, traders are advised to keep an eye out for crypto-related news and act accordingly. Information is key here.

OTHER CONSIDERATIONS

Trading cryptocurrencies can be nerve-wracking because there are so many things you need to consider and many things that can go wrong. Trading in itself is quite complex, and trading crypto is even more so. It is not for everyone, especially beginners.

If you are new to the whole trading game, you should at least come equipped with some knowledge about trading. We have covered some of it here but there is so much more to explore.

What can you do, then? There are many free resources out there to help you understand the trading game, such as BabyPips. Some of their content might not be totally accurate and their specialty is in forex, but most of the concepts apply to crypto trading as well. The content you find online might be inaccurate or outdated, however.

This is why you should also consider signing up for some classes. Sure, you have to pay a bit of cash and some of them are outright scams, but if you do your research, you should be able to find some reputable mentors who can show you the ropes. Consider this an investment that will pay you much more in the future.

Other than that, never start trading with your own money straight away. Most trading platforms offer demo accounts with a certain amount of virtual balance. You get to play with real-world live data and trade with this virtual cash and see how you are doing. This is a great place to practice and familiarize yourself with the trading platform. Once you are comfortable, you can then put money in and start to trade for real.

CONCLUSION

We are now at the end of our second book in our three-book series on cryptocurrency and how to trade and invest in it. We have covered a lot of things here, but this is by no means an exhaustive coverage of the vast world of cryptocurrency.

In short, cryptocurrency is considered to be a digital asset and there are many forms of cryptocurrencies. Its biggest selling point is the fact that it is a decentralized currency, which is free from the control of major financial institutions and governments. This is important because many people are tired of the government putting their tax money into all the wrong causes or industries that are not important are getting bailed out while the common folks have to deal with the economic hardships. This is very apparent in the Great Depression and the recent Covid pandemic.

So, with the Bitcoin halving, there is renewed interest in cryptocurrency once again. While some speculated that it is merely a bubble that would pop again just like it did the last time in the 2016 halving, others believe that this time will be different. This is because more and more companies and major financial institutions are getting in on the action.

It’s not just cryptocurrencies that have future potential. The technology behind it is also quite versatile. Blockchain technology has so much potential that even if cryptocurrencies were to fade into obscurity tomorrow, blockchain would still be around. It created smart contracts, decentralized apps, and decentralized financial technologies, all of which would be very helpful in any given industry. It also provides a way to store information that is very secure against hackers, if set up properly. In this case, cryptocurrency would just be tokens to enable these services.

As with anything in life, trading or investing in cryptocurrency is never a straightforward process. At least, not if you want to succeed. There are so many things to consider that would give anyone a headache.

Of course, you can just invest in Bitcoin and Ethereum and hold onto those coins until they reach a certain point before you sell it all off. But there is more to cryptocurrency investment than this.

For one, you can put your Ether into startups or projects that launch ICOs or STOs. If their projects are successful, you can sell off the utility tokens to make an even bigger profit or hold onto those so you can access various services. For security tokens, you basically hold a share in the company and you can hold onto these tokens and have a solid source of passive revenue.

Of course, it is not just Bitcoin and Ethereum that are in the crypto space. They are the big players, sure, but there are also many other cryptocurrencies to consider. And you should really consider investing in Altcoins. There is no telling when any of the big players will suddenly crash in value because of government regulations or something else, so you need to be diversifying your investment.

What is really interesting about the crypto world is that different cryptocurrencies and tokens are created for different purposes. While Bitcoin is intended to be the digital gold, Ethereum aims to provide a platform for developers to create decentralized applications. Other cryptocurrencies attempt to facilitate low-cost and quick transactions, file sharing, or video streaming. All of this is thanks to the wide range of applications of the underlying blockchain technology.

And you should really consider the endless possibility in the crypto world. The developers in that space are coming up with new and creative ways to solve problems. They have different solutions for the same issue, and this only encourages new technological development.

For you as an investor or trader, there is a lot of money to be made here, provided that you know what you are doing. Unfortunately, a lot of people in the crypto world are not familiar with trading. They think that trading or investing in cryptocurrencies is a way to get rich quickly without fully understanding the intricacies of trading in the first place. So, they end up losing a lot of money and this gives cryptocurrency a bad reputation.

Mastering the art of trade is not easy, but there is profit to be made. It takes careful consideration, experience, and a little bit of luck to pull off. But if you keep your wits about you and diligently gather information and do your research, and make your decisions are based on facts, not other people’s opinions, you are well on your way to seeing your equity in a beautiful bright green color.

Of course, the story does not end there because there are taxes to consider. If you can afford it, the best thing to do would be to get a certified public accountant to help you. They know their way around the tax laws and can give you some advice on how you can minimize your tax. Their service is quite pricey, but it pays itself with the deduced tax rate over time. Just make sure that your CPA is familiar with cryptocurrency and has experience in dealing with it.

Even if you are not interested in investing in cryptocurrency, you should at least consider the future potential for the underlying blockchain technology that powers cryptocurrencies.

And this is part of the reason why large corporations are getting in on the action, especially the giants from Silicon Valley. They realize the potential application for blockchain technology, which is virtually limitless, and they understand how they can leverage this to bring new innovations to the world.

We are now starting to see wider adoption for cryptocurrency. Governments across the globe are working on ways to regulate cryptocurrency as well. Things are really looking up for cryptocurrency, despite the naysayers.

Even if you do not want to invest in cryptocurrency, they are the very thing that powers blockchain-enabled applications. They provide some unique and interesting solutions in the market and some of them are really starting to take off. Indeed, experts are still divided on whether cryptocurrency is here to stay, but you need to consider the future potential for blockchain technology. Being an early adopter of cryptocurrency is risky, but the reward is much better than the associated risks.

If there is only one thing that you can take away from this entire book, it would be that any investment decision, be it in cryptocurrency or anything else, needs to be backed up by solid data and information. Anyone has an opinion and they can say anything they want. But nothing beats cold and concrete facts. Information is key, so do your research and make your decisions from there.
